Observed: core Site Settings reload fails after save; new STAFF enrollment/login fails. Owner media and security actions pass. Restored public theme/readiness pass; anonymous protected integrations/intake deny, private unreferenced media and production smoke return 404. Authenticated cross-site/object matrices and Lean/Standard coverage remain open.

All snapshots below are historical. Their PASS, VERIFIED, source SHA and count statements apply only to their recorded scope and do not override RC-08C.

# Route matrix

Source candidate: `e24fc53d9e370e28f01398c561b0f5adc4884756`. Reconciled 2026-10-03. Initial working tree was clean. This documentation freeze binds the source candidate; its artifact commit is discoverable with git log -- docs/rc. It does not establish runtime release readiness.

All filesystem page/route handlers, configured custom views, before-nav links, and application collection/global route families are included. Dynamic segments are route families, not a claim that each record exists. Payload REST/GraphQL catchalls expose only gated registrations and their access policy. Duplicate URLs deliberately retain separate frontend/custom-view/navigation implementations: precedence and composition need browser acceptance. Test references indicate source coverage candidates only; none ran here. Generated endpoints such as collection create/edit inherit their family row.

## RC-01 current admin audit — 2026-10-03

Base HEAD: `6818864c9e601860d6a7c7874ae02fd0b7fdb48d`; working-tree repairs are uncommitted. This appendix supersedes historical admin browser evidence only; it does not promote any RC-00 registration to VERIFIED.

The inventory generator imports current sanitized Payload config and renders both before-nav components against its actual registered collections. Resource profile and module selection are independent. Lean/floor and Standard/floor: 16 custom views, 16 links (including View Site), 12 visible collections, one global. Lean/all and Standard/all: 16 views, 24 links, 18 visible collections, two globals. JSON snapshots preserve every hidden/conditional registration and configured component. Custom-view registration remains unconditional; optional direct-view safety is still BLOCKER.

The data-driven matrix owns 42 distinct configured/visible admin route targets. Additional static links from configured workspaces below are discovery candidates; browser reachability is not inferred from source. Dynamic record links and nested action controls still require ordinary record workflows. Supported Payload roles are owner, administrator and staff; editor/author/moderator are not alternate Payload role values.

## RC-02 consolidated journey ? PASS ? 2026-10-03

Source `b684ee11460637bf2177f349057f14d6e804bbbd`. [Executed assertions/trace](evidence/RC-02.md). This appendix supersedes historical ?not executed? entries only for the actions below, not broader roles/CRUD/provider scope.

| Surface                                                                                | Proof                                                                                                         |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `/setup`, `/admin`                                                                     | Empty install, owner/passkey, admin access                                                                    |
| Settings, Theme Studio, media library, content editor, `/builder/[id]`                 | Saved settings/tokens/theme, binary DAM, native rich-text save and revision preview, Puck composition/publish |
| Quality Scans / Rendered Quality                                                       | Real worker completion; nine actual HTTP pages, zero warning/blocking findings                                |
| `/`, `/about`, `/get-involved`, `/articles`, two article routes, event, category/topic | Anonymous 200, navigation/mobile, metadata/schema/related reporting                                           |
| `/search`                                                                              | Title/body/taxonomy results; unpublished body excluded until republish                                        |
| `/sitemap.xml`, sitemap child, `/robots.txt`, `/feed.xml`, `/feed.json`                | 200; private/internal layouts excluded                                                                        |
| Old article slug / absent route                                                        | Exact 308 + `/articles/republic-public-records`; 404                                                          |
| Private draft / unused private media / anonymous admin data                            | Denied or absent                                                                                              |
| Fresh public requests after web + worker restart                                       | Page/article/event/media/sitemap/feed usable                                                                  |

Author byline and Person schema passed; no clickable author archive is advertised in the current renderer. Transcript search is not claimed for the created article/event content.

## RC-03 Pass 2 route boundary

Current acceptance is limited to `/admin/workflow`, native content revision editing, `/admin/releases`, `/admin/social`, native social account configuration, Events admin and canonical public content/redirect/event routes. Unsupported calendar/timeline product collection operations return 403, `/api/calendar/export` returns 410, and `/calendar` shows its explicit RC deferral. See [RC-03 evidence](evidence/RC-03.md) for execution verdict. Historical full-role/tenant route matrix remains open.
